- **update-all-bc-agents**: All-hands re-orientation for the BC (Blip.Company) team. Use after substantive doc changes that the entire BC team needs to absorb. Trigger on "/update-all-bc-agents", "update all BC agents", "all-hands re-orient BC", "broadcast new docs to BC team", "BC team re-onboard". Do NOT mix with QC — BC has its own source docs and scope. ([miteproyects/opensankey](https://github.com/miteproyects/opensankey/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/update-all-bc-agents) · ★1)
